- **vsdd**: Verification-Driven System Design. Use when designing systems, architectures, APIs, data models, service boundaries, or any cross-cutting structural decision. - **vsdd-phase-1-requirements**: Drive VSDD Phase 1 — Requirements Refinement. - **vsdd-phase-2-spec**: Drive VSDD Phase 2 — Spec Crystallization. Encode the approved SRS into a technical SDD (behavioural contract + verification architecture) per work item, then clear Gate 2. - **vsdd-phase-3-tests-impl**: Drive VSDD Phase 3 — Test Generation & TDD Implementation. - **vsdd-phase-4-adversarial**: Drive VSDD Phase 4 — Adversarial Refinement. - **vsdd-phase-5-feedback**: Drive VSDD Phase 5 — Feedback Integration Loop. - **vsdd-phase-6-hardening**: Drive VSDD Phase 6 — Formal Hardening. Invoke after Gate 4 clears, or when the user runs verification / hardening. ([adamdaw/vsdd](https://github.com/adamdaw/vsdd/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/vsdd-phase-6-hardening) · ★2)
- **vsdd-phase-7-convergence**: Drive VSDD Phase 7 — Convergence.
